118:1 | 118 (H1/3, p. 826). PRAISE). (Introversions and Alternations.)
118:1 | the LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II. mercy = lovingkindness, or grace.
118:5 | THE LORD. Heb. Jah. Ap. 4. III. and set me in a large place. The current Heb. text = with the deliverance of JAH, bammerhab yah (two words). The Massoretic text reads it as one word, bammerhabyah = with deliverance. The A.V. and R.V. transfer the yah to the beginning of the clause, and are then compelled to make out the sense by supplying "and set me". They do not even notice the Massoretic reading. The printed text reads: "I called upon Jah in distress, He answered me with the deliverance of Yah." The Massoretic text reads: "I called upon Yah in distress, He answered me with deliverance."
118:6 | The LORD, &c. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II. Quoted in Heb. 13. 6. man = a man. Heb. 'adam. Ap. 14. I.
118:8 | It is better . . . Than. Fig. Coenotes (Ap. 6), repeated in v. 9. better = good. By Fig. Heterosis (Ap. 6), the Positive is put for the Comparative, and is so rendered. trust = flee for refuge. Heb. hasah. Ap. 69. II. put confidence. Heb. batah. Ap. 69. I. man. Heb. 'adam. Ap. 14. I.
118:10 | All. Put by Fig. Synecdoche (of Genus), Ap. 6, for a large number, or many. But, &c. Fig. Epistrophe (Ap. 6), repeated in v. 11.
118:11 | They compassed . . . they compassed. Fig. Epizeuxis (Ap. 6), for emphasis.
118:12 | They compassed. Fig. Anaphora (Ap. 6), repeated from v. 11. are quenched. Sept. reads "blazed up". the name. See note on 20. 1.
118:13 | Thou. Does this refer to the "man" of v. 6?
118:14 | song. Put by Fig. Metonymy (of Adjunct), Ap. 6, for the theme of the song. salvation. Cp. v. 21. Ex. 15. 2. Isa. 12. 2. Put by Fig. Metonymy (of Cause), Ap. 6, for Him Who saves = my Saviour.
118:15 | tabernacles = tents, or dwellings. Heb. 'ohel. Ap. 40 (3). The right hand. Fig. Anthropopatheia. Ap. 6. doeth valiantly. Fig. Coenotes (Ap. 6), repeated in v. 16.
118:17 | works. Some codices, with one early printed edition, read "work" (sing.).
118:18 | chastened me sore. The Fig. Polyptoton (Ap. 6), thus well rendered. Heb. = "chastening He chastened me".
118:22 | The stone: i. e. the Messiah. See Gen. 49. 24. A stone of stumbling, Isa. 8. 14 (cp. Rom. 9. 33. 1 Pet. 2. 8); a "tried stone", "precious", "sure", Isa. 28. 16; the rejected stone (cp. Matt. 21. 42. Mark 12. 10, 11. Luke 20. 17. Acts 4. 11. 1 Pet. 2. 4). The true foundation, Isa. 28. 16 (cp. Matt. 16. 18. 1 Cor. 3. 11. Eph. 2. 20). refused. See note above, and cp. Structure, "i" with "i", above. Here the present Dispensation comes in. See Ap. 72.
118:23 | This is the LORD'S doing. Messiah's exaltation is thus like the humiliation (109. 27).
118:25 | Save now, &c. Heb. "Hosanna" = Save, I pray. Not a Particle of time, but of entreaty (as in Ecc. 12. 1). Repeated four times for emphasis. Lit. "I pray Thee, Jehovah; Save; I pray Thee; I pray Thee, O Jehovah".
118:26 | Blessed, &c. See Matt. 21. 9; 23. 39. Mark 11. 9. Luke 13. 35; 19. 38. John 12. 13. you. Plural.
118:27 | GOD. Heb. El. Ap. 4. IV. Bind. Heb. 'asar, to bind, or join. Here, in its idiomatic usage, to join, so as to make ready (Gen. 46. 29. Ex. 14. 6. 1 Kings 18. 44. 2 Kings 9. 21), or begin (1 Kings 20. 14. 2 Chron. 13. 3). sacrifice. Heb. hag = a feast, or festal [sacrifice]. See note on Ex. 23. 18, and cp. Ex. 5. 1; 12. 14; 23. 14. Lev. 23. 39, 41. Num. 29. 12. Deut. 16. 15. Zech. 14. 16, 18, 19. cords = wreaths, or garlands, as in Ex. 28. 14, 22, 24, 25; 39. 15, 17, 18. even unto. Heb. 'ad = up to or during: i. e. even until [it is consummated at] the horns of the altar. 'Ad denotes progression in time. Translate: "Make ready the festal sacrifice with garlands until [it is consummated at] the horns of the altar." Cp. Acts 14. 13. There is nothing about "to the altar" here.
118:28 | praise = give thanks. God. Heb. Elohim. Ap. 4. I.

119:91 | They: i. e. heaven and earth. continue = stand, as in v. 90. this day = [to] this day, or to-day. ordinances = regulations. Heb. mishpat. The seventh of the ten words. Ap. 73. Cp. v. 132. all. With Art. = the whole [universe].
119:94 | sought = inquired into, or studied. Cp. 105. 4.
119:96 | all perfection = an end, or limit to all things. Cp. Job 26. 10; 28. 3. Thy commandment, &c. = spacious exceedingly [are] Thy commandments: i. e. including all (as opposed to "end").
119:97 | 97-104 (מ Mem). JEHOVAH'S WORD THE SOURCE OF WISDOM. (Introversion and Alternations.)
119:98 | 98-100 (Y, above). THE SOURCE OF UNDERSTANDING (Pos.). (Repeated Alternation.)
119:98 | through: or, as to. they are = it [is]: i. e. the Law containing the commandments.
119:99 | than all my teachers. Because Divinely taught Divine wisdom.
119:100 | ancients = elders, or the aged ones.
119:101 | evil. Heb. ra'a'. Ap. 44. viii. way. Including religious way, in the sense of Acts 9. 2; 19. 9, 23; 24. 14. Cp. Acts 16. 17; 18. 26; especially in v. 104. might. A.V., 1611, reads "may". word. Same word as in v. 9. Some codices, with one early printed edition, with Aram., Sept., Syr., and Vulg., read "words" (pl.).
119:102 | departed = swerved. taught = directed.
119:103 | sweet = smooth, or agreeable. Not the same word as in 19. 10.
119:105 | 105-112 (נ Nun). JEHOVAH'S WORD THE SOURCE OF LIGHT AND JOY. (Introversion and Alternation.)
119:105 | lamp: or lantern, for light on the path for the feet; not merely a light for the eyes (19. 8).
119:106 | I will perform it. Some cod., with seven early printed editions, Aram., Sept., Syr., and Vulg., read "I have performed it"; but some cod., with four early printed editions (1 in marg.), read "and will perform it".
119:109 | in my hand. An idiom for great danger. Cp. Judg. 12. 3. 1 Sam. 19. 5; 28. 21. Job 13. 14.
119:110 | erred = to err from the paths of virtue and piety. Heb. ta'ah; not the same word as in vv. 21, 118.
119:113 | 113-120 (ס Samech). JEHOVAH'S LAW. THE SECURITY GIVEN BY IT. (Introversion.)
119:113 | thoughts = divided or doubting thoughts. Same root as 1 Kings 18. 21 (cp. Jas. 1. 8); or, them that are of double mind.
119:114 | hiding place. Cp. 32. 7; 91. 1. shield. Cp. 84. 9, and note. Fig. Anthropopatheia. Ap. 6.
119:115 | God. Heb. Elohim. Ap. 4. I. But "my God" implies Jehovah.
119:116 | live. See note ‖ on p. 827.
119:117 | and I shall be safe = so shall I be saved. have respect unto. Aram. and Syr. read "find dear delight in". Sept. reads "shall meditate".
119:118 | trodden down: or set at naught.
119:119 | Thou puttest away. Sept. and Vulg. read "I have accounted".
119:120 | My flesh trembleth: or, My flesh creeps (as we say). Cp. Job 4. 15: i. e. at the judgment executed on the wicked.
119:121 | 121-128 (ע Ain). JEHOVAH'S SERVANT'S CONFIDENCE. (Extended Alternation.)
119:121 | justice = righteousness. Same word as in v. 7.
119:122 | proud = arrogant. This is the only verse in this Psalm which has not one of the "ten words", unless we may include the Living Word Himself, Who is the "surety" for His people. See note on Prov. 11. 15, and cp. Heb. 7. 22. See Ap. 73.
119:124 | mercy = lovingkindness, or grace.
119:126 | to work: i. e. to intervene.
119:129 | 129-136 (פ Pe). GUIDANCE BY THE WORD. (Introversion.)
119:129 | wonderful. Same root as in vv. 18, 27. keep = keep safely.
119:130 | entrance = doorway; which was always an open way for the light, in the absence of windows. simple = sincere, as opposed to crafty.
119:131 | I longed. An Aramaic word (ya'ab). Occurs only here. Weaker than in vv. 20, 40, 174.
119:132 | merciful = gracious. As Thou usest to do = According to Thy ordinance. Heb. mishpat. The seventh in order of the "ten words" (Ap. 73). So rendered in v. 91. name. See note on 20. 1.
119:133 | Order = Direct, or guide. in = by. Some codices, with one early printed edition, Sept., and Vulg., read "according to". iniquity. Heb. 'aven. Ap. 44. iii.
119:134 | Deliver: i. e. by power. Heb. padah. See notes on Ex. 6. 6 and 13. 13. Not the same word as vv. 153, 154, 170. man. Heb. 'adam. Ap. 14. I.
119:136 | Rivers of waters. Heb. palgey-mayim. See note on Prov. 21. 1. Eng. idiom = Floods of tears.
119:137 | 137-144 (צ Tzaddi). JEHOVAH'S WORD AND THE PSALMIST. (Repeated Alternation.)
119:137 | Righteous, &c. See v. 7. See also Rev. 16. 5, 7.
119:138 | righteous and very faithful = righteousness and faithfulness.
119:140 | very pure = refined.
119:141 | small = insignificant. Cp. Judg. 6. 15.
119:142 | is the truth = is truth (no Art.). Cp. John 17. 17.
119:143 | taken hold. Fig. Prosopopoeia. Ap. 6.
119:145 | 145-152 (ק Koph.) 153-160 (ר Resh.) PRAYER FOR FAITHFULNESS IN DISTRESS. (Extended Alternation.)
119:145 | cried = called [unto Thee], as in v. 146. hear = answer.
119:146 | cried = called. Fig. Anaphora. Ap. 6.
119:147 | prevented = anticipated, or forestalled. dawning. A Homonym. Heb. nesheph. See note on 1 Sam. 30. 17. A.V. and R.V. correctly render it here, and in Job 7. 4; though not in 1 Sam. 30. 17. Job 24. 15. word. Same as in v. 11; but some codices, with Sept. and Vulg., read "words" (pl.).
119:148 | prevent = anticipate, or forestall. watches. See Ap. 51. III. 4 (18), p. 74.
119:149 | Hear. Emphatic = O do hear.
119:150 | after mischief. Some codices, with Sept., Syr., and Vulg., read "after me maliciously".
119:153 | deliver. Twenty-five Heb. words so rendered. Here, halaz = rescue (with a gentle hand); not the same word as in vv. 134, 154, 170.
119:154 | deliver. Heb. ga'al = redeem. See notes on Ex. 6. 6 and 13. 13. Not the same word as in vv. 134, 153, 170.
119:156 | tender mercies = compassions.
119:157 | enemies = adversaries: i. e. those who hem me in.
119:158 | transgressors. Heb. bagad = traitors, or treacherous men. was grieved = loathed myself: i. e. seeing the same tendencies in myself. Cp. Job 42. 6.
119:160 | beginning. Heb. r'osh = head. Put by Fig. Synecdoche (of Part), Ap. 6, for the whole (including the beginning, and "every one", as in next line) = sum and substance, word and words (Jer. 15. 16. John 17. 8, 14); rendered "sum" in 139. 17.
119:161 | 161-168 (ש Schin). JEHOVAH'S WORD GIVES PEACE AND COMFORT. (Repeated Alternation.)
119:161 | Princes = Rulers. word. Same word as in v. 9. Some codices, with Sept. and Vulg., read "words" (pl.).
119:162 | word. Same word as in v. 11. Some codices with Sept. and Vulg., read "words" (pl.).
119:163 | lying = falsehood = what is false; especially false religion and idolatry.
119:164 | Seven times. Not a "round" number, but the number of spiritual perfection. See Ap. 10.
119:165 | peace. Put by Fig. Synecdoche (of Part), Ap. 6, for every blessing connected with peace. offend them = make them stumble.
119:169 | 169-176 (ת Tau). PETITIONS AND STATEMENTS. (Repeated Alternation.)
119:170 | Deliver = Rescue. Heb. nazal, to pluck out of the hands of an enemy; recover. Not the same word as in vv. 134, 153, 154.
119:171 | utter = pour forth or bubble over with. Cp. Prov. 15. 2; 18. 4.
119:172 | speak of. Heb. respond with. Put by Fig. Synecdoche (of Species), Ap. 6, for all kinds of speaking or singing; hence = praise.
119:173 | hand. Fig. Anthropopatheia (Ap. 6); "hand" put by Fig. Metonymy (of Cause), Ap. 6, for power exercised by it.
119:175 | it shall praise Thee. Refers to Isa. 38. 20. help me. Refers to Isa. 37. 33-36.
119:176 | lost = perishing. Cp. Matt. 18. 11; Luke 19. 10.
120:1 | 120 (G2/1, p. 826). GROUP I. PSALM (FIRST). DISTRESS. (Introversion.)
120:1 | Title. A Song. Heb. shir. See Ap. 65. XXIII: viz. one of the Songs promised by Hezekiah in Isa. 38. 20. degrees = the degrees, or steps. Heb. hamma'aloth (with Art.): i. e. the "degrees" mentioned six times in 2 Kings 20. 8-11, and five times in Isa. 38. 8 (Heb.). No other "degrees" known to Scripture which are connected with the shadow of the sun. For the origin, authorship, examination, and Structure, see Ap. 67, and note § on p. 827.
120:1 | distress. The first Psalm of each of the five groups speaks of Distress; the second of Trust; the third of Blessing and Peace in Zion. The distress, here, refers to Sennacherib's siege of Jerusalem (2 Kings 19. 3. Isa. 37. 3) cried. See 2 Kings 19. 3, 4, 14-19. 2 Chron. 32. 20. Isa. 37. 15-20; 38. 2, 3. See Ap. 67. iv. the LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II. heard = answered.
120:2 | Deliver = Pluck me. Heb. nazal. Same word as in 119. 170. The reference is to 2 Kings 18. 30, 32. my soul = me. Heb. nephesh. Ap. 13. lips. Heb. = lip: i. e. Rab-shakeh's. The reference is to 2 Kings 18. 19-35; 19. 8-13. 2 Chron. 32. 10-19. Isa. 36. 4-20; 37. 8-13. See Ap. 67. i. tongue. Fig. Epistrophe (Ap. 6), with "tongue", v. 3.
120:3 | done = heaped upon: i. e. added to. Cp. 1 Sam. 3. 17; 20. 13, &c.
120:4 | mighty = Mighty [One].
120:5 | Mesech . . . Kedar. Used typically of cruel and merciless peoples; as we use the terms Vandals, Goths, Philistines.
120:6 | him. Some codices, with Sept., Syr., and Vulg., read "them".
120:7 | I = I [even I]; or, I [am all] peace (emphatic). speak = speak [of peace]. See note on 109. 4. they are for war. The reference is to 2 Kings 18. 19. 2 Chron. 32. 2. Isa. 36. 5.
121:1 | 121 (G2/2, p. 826). GROUP I. PSALM (SECOND). TRUST. (Division.)
121:1 | Title. A Song. Heb. shir. See Ap. 65. XXIII. of degrees = for, or relating to the degrees. Only here thus. Heb. lamma'aloth. See note on Title of 120.
121:1 | 1, 2 (C1, above). JEHOVAH'S HELP PROCLAIMED. (Introversion.)
121:1 | hills = mountains. Add a full stop. From whence, &c.? Punctuate this line as a question. Cp. Jer. 3. 23. cometh = is to come.
121:2 | My help. Fig. Anadiplosis, repeated from end of v. 1. the LORD = Jehovah (Ap. 4. II), not the hills. Which made heaven and earth. The reference is to the burden of Hezekiah's prayer (2 Kings 19. 15. Isa. 37 16). Idols were only the work of men's hands (2 Kings 19. 18. 2 Chron. 32. 19. Isa. 37. 19). Rab-shakeh had reproached "the living God". See further references to this in 124. 8; 134. 3; and Ap. 67. v.
121:3 | 3-8 (C2, p. 845). JEHOVAH'S HELP. PROMISED. (Alternation.)
121:3 | not = May He not. Heb. 'al (like Gr. me). (Subjective, and conditional). keepeth . . . 4 keepeth . . . 5 keeper. Note the Fig. Polyptoton (Ap. 6). Heb. shomreka . . . shomer . . . shomreka. Repeated in vv. 7, 8.
121:4 | neither. Heb. l'o (like Gr. ou). He will not. Absolute.
121:7 | shall preserve thee. The repeated promise of Jehovah by Isaiah (2 Kings 19. 20-34. Isa. 37. 6, 7, 22-35. shall preserve . . . shall preserve . . . 8 shall preserve. Note the Fig. Polyptoton (Ap. 6). Heb. yishmarka . . . yishmor . . . yishmar. evil = calamity. Heb. ra'a'. Ap. 44. viii. soul. Heb. nephesh. Ap. 13. See Isa. 38.
121:8 | thy going out, &c. Idiom for life in general. The promise was fulfilled in 2 Chron. 32. 22.
122:1 | 122 (G2/3, p. 826). GROUP I. PSALM (THIRD). DELIVERANCE, BLESSING, AND PEACE IN ZION. (Introversion.)
122:1 | Title. A Song. Heb. shir. See note on Title of Ps. 120, and Ap. 65. XXIII. of degrees = of the degrees (with Art.), as in Title of 120. See Ap. 67, and note on p. 827. of David = by David. A Psalm which Hezekiah found ready to his hand. Some codices, with Aram. and Syr., omit "of David".
122:1 | the house of the LORD. Heb. the house of Jehovah (Ap. 4. II). This was Hezekiah's constant care, desire, and thought. It filled his heart. He began his reign by "opening its doors" and cleansing it. See 2 Chron. 29—31, where it is mentioned seventeen times. He spread Sennacherib's letter before Jehovah there (Isa. 37. 14). In his mortal sickness his prayer and its answer related to it (2 Kings 20. 5). The "sign" he asked related to it (2 Kings 20. 8. Isa. 38. 22). His songs were to be sung there (Isa. 38. 20). See Ap. 67. xiii. the LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II.
122:2 | shall stand = have stood [and shall still stand.] The reference is to the Passover, which had been kept for "all Israel". See Ap. 67. xv. Jerusalem. Note the Fig. Anadiplosis (Ap. 6), the word being repeated at the beginning of the next verse.
122:3 | compact = coupled together (as by a bridge), as Moriah was joined with Zion by the Millo. See note on 1 Kings 9. 15; 2 Kings 12. 20, and Ap. 68.
122:4 | go up. See Ap. 68. "Zion". THE LORD. Heb. Jah. Ap. 4. III. testimony: the Ark of Jehovah. name. See note on 20. 1.
122:5 | thrones. Pl. of Majesty = the great Throne.
122:6 | Pray for the peace of Jerusalem. Fig. Paronomasia (Ap. 6), sha'alu shelom yerushalam yishlayu. See Ap. 67. vi.
122:7 | Peace be within. Fig. Epanadiplosis (Ap. 6), uniting vv. 7 and 8 by beginning and ending with the same words.
122:8 | say = speak [saying].
122:9 | God. Heb. Elohim. Ap. 4. I.
123:1 | 123 (G2/4, p. 826). GROUP II. PSALM (FIRST). DISTRESS. (Introversion.)
123:1 | Title. A Song, &c. Same as Ps. 120.
123:1 | dwellest in the heavens. The reference is to 2 Kings 19. 15 and Isa. 37. 16.
123:2 | Behold. Fig. Asterismos. Ap. 6. as. Fig. Simile. Ap. 6. the LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II. God. Heb. Elohim. Ap. 4. I. have mercy = Be gracious. Fig. Anaphora. Ap. 6.
123:3 | contempt = the mockery. Cp. 119. 22, referring to Rab-shakeh.
123:4 | Our soul = we. Heb. nephesh (Ap. 13). scorning = the scoffing. The reference is to the scoffing of Sennacherib and Rab-shakeh (2 Kings 18. 19-35; 19. 8-13. 2 Chron. 32. 10-19. Isa. 36. 4-21; 37. 8-13). Ap. 67. i. at ease. Same Heb. as "tumult" in 2 Kings 19. 28, and Isa. 37. 29. proud = proud oppressors.
124:1 | 124 (G2/5, p. 826). GROUP II. PSALM (SECOND). TRUST. (Introversion and Alternation.)
124:1 | Title. See note on Title of Ps. 120.
124:1 | the LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II. Now. Refers to Hezekiah's deliverance. See Ap. 67. x.
124:2 | men. Heb. 'adam. Ap. 14. I. (Sing. refers to Sennacherib).
124:3 | they. The pl., referring to Sennacherib's hosts; likened to a stream and waters in vv. 4, 5. See note on Ps. 46. 3. quick = alive.
124:4 | stream = torrent, or flood. Heb. nahal. See Ap. 67. our soul = us. Heb. nephesh. Ap. 13. Note the Fig. Epistrophe (Ap. 6) in the repetition at end of v. 5.
124:7 | as a bird. The reference is to the words of Sennacherib on his cylinder, where he mentions Hezekiah by name, whom he had got "as a bird in a cage". See Ap. 67. xi.
124:8 | name. See note on 20. 1. Who made heaven and earth. See Ap. 67. v, and note on 121. 2 and 134. 3.
125:1 | 125 (G2/6, p. 826). GROUP II. PSALM (THIRD). BLESSING AND PEACE IN ZION. (Introversion.)
125:1 | Title. Same as Ps. 120. See Ap. 67.
125:1 | trust = confide. Heb. batah. Ap. 69. I. the LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II. Shall be as mount Zion. Some codices, with one early printed edition and Syr., read "are in Mount Zion". Ap. 68. for ever. Note the Fig. Epistrophe (Ap. 6), the words being repeated at the end of the next line.
125:3 | For = Surely. rod, or cudgel. the wicked = the wicked (or lawless) one. Heb. rasha'. Ap. 44. x. Here the reference is to Sennacherib (Isa. 30. 31), but it looks forward to 2 Thess. 2. 3, 4. rest upon = continue over. lot = heritage (as allotted). righteous. Pl.: i. e. Hezekiah and the godly in Israel. iniquity. Heb. 'aval. Ap. 44. vi.

126:1 | 126 (G2/7, p. 826). GROUP III. PSALM (FIRST). DISTRESS. (Extended Alternation.)
126:1 | Title. Same as Ps. 120.
126:1 | the LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II. turned . . . the captivity = turned the fortunes. This does not refer to a captivity or captives, but to a restoration to blessing. See Job 42. 10 and Ezek. 16. 53 and 55, where it is three times explained as "return to your former estate". See Ap. 67. xii. Zion. See Ap. 68. like them that dream. The reference is to the waking in 2 Kings 19. 35. Isa. 37. 36. The illustration is in Luke 24. 41. Acts 12. 9 (603 B.C.).
126:2 | singing: i. e. the songs of Isa. 38. 20. said they = was it said. heathen = nations. The reference is to 2 Chron. 32. 22, 23. The LORD hath done great things. Fig. Anadiplosis (Ap. 6), because the phrase is repeated at the beginning of the next verse.
126:4 | streams = torrents. Heb. 'aphikim. See 2 Sam. 22. 16. Supply the Ellipsis, "as the streams [are turned] in the Negeb". in the south = in the Negeb, where, in the hill-country of Judaea the 'aphikim are turned about in their beds between the rocks and in the gorges. south. Heb. Negeb; the hill-country of Judaea. See note on Gen. 13. 1, and Deut. 1. 7.
126:5 | sow in tears. The reference is to the "sign" given in Isa. 37. 30. See Ap. 67. ix.
127:1 | 127 (G2/8, p. 826). GROUP III. PSALM (SECOND). TRUST. (Alternation.)
127:1 | Title. A Song of degrees. Same as 120 ("the degrees"). Ap. 67. The Structure, and the references to Hezekiah being childless (Ap. 67. xiv), show that this is not a Psalm "made up of two smaller Psalms, having no connection with each other". for Solomon = of or by Solomon. The central Psalm of the fifteen. Selected by Hezekiah to complete and perfect the arrangement.
127:1 | the LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II. the = a.
127:2 | For so = Thus. beloved = beloved one (sing.). Heb. yedid. This was Solomon's name (Jedidiah) given by Jehovah (2 Sam. 12. 25). Solomon was given because David was beloved of Jehovah. Some codices, with Sept., Syr., and Vulg., read pl. sleep = in sleep: i. e. while they sleep: i. e. without their labour. So He gave to Solomon (1 Kings 3. 5-15); to Adam (Gen. 2. 21, 22); Abraham (Gen. 15. 12, 13); Jacob (Gen. 28. 10-15); Samuel (1 Sam. 3. 3, 4), &c.
127:3 | Lo. Fig. Asterismos. Ap. 6. children = sons. The reference to the fact that Hezekiah was rejoicing in Isaiah's message that he should have a son, made it a suitable Psalm for Hezekiah to select (2 Kings 20. 12, 18. Isa. 39. 7). Cp. Ps. 128. See Ap. 67. xiv. When his trouble came, he was childless. Hence he remembered Jehovah's promise to David (132. 11). of = from. Hence he sings Jehovah's praise.
127:5 | Happy is the man. Hezekiah was that man. See the Beatitudes. Ap. 63. VI. man. Heb. geber. Ap. 14. IV. They: i. e. the sons. not be ashamed. Fig. Tapeinosis (Ap. 6): quite the opposite. speak = meet, whether for negotiation or for fighting.
128:1 | 128 (G2/9, p. 826). GROUP III. PSALM (THIRD). DELIVERANCE, BLESSING, AND PEACE. (Alternations.)
128:1 | Title. A Song of degrees. Same as 120. See Ap. 67.
128:1 | Blessed is = O the happinesses of. See the Beatitudes. Ap. 63. VI. feareth = revereth. the LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II.
128:2 | labour. Put by Fig. Metonymy (of Cause), Ap. 6, for that which is produced by labour. thine hands: i. e. thine own hands, in contrast with the opposite (Lev. 26. 16. Deut. 28. 30-33, 39, 40). See also Amos 5. 11. Mic. 6. 15.
128:3 | wife . . . fruitful. The reference is, as in Ps. 127, to the fact that Hezekiah was childless at this time and longed for an heir. See Ap. 67. xiv. children = sons.
128:4 | Behold. Fig. Asterismos. Ap. 6. man = a strong man (pl.). Heb. geber. Ap. 14. IV. blessed. Not the same word as in v. 1. That is happy (Beatitudo); this is blessed (Benedictio).
128:5 | And thou shalt see: or, That thou mayest see.
128:6 | thou shalt see. Hezekiah did see. peace. Because this Psalm concludes a group.
129:1 | 129 (G2/10, p. 826). GROUP IV. PSALM (FIRST). DISTRESS. (Alternation.)
129:1 | Title. A Song of degrees. Same as 120. See Ap. 67.
129:2 | Many a time. Fig. Anaphora (Ap. 6), being repeated from v. 1. Yet = Nevertheless. Heb. gam, as in 119. 24 ("also"); Ezek. 16. 28. Ecc. 6. 7. Not "reduplicated by mistake, and then spelt differently to make sense", as is alleged by modern criticism.
129:3 | The plowers. No Art.
129:4 | The LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II. righteous = just: i. e. in His judgments. cords: i. e. of bondage. Cp. 2. 3. the wicked = lawless ones. Heb. rasha'. Ap. 44. x.
129:5 | Zion. See Ap. 68.
129:6 | as the grass = as grass. The reference in vv. 6, 7 is not to "Egyptian monuments", but to the reply of Jehovah concerning Sennacherib, which Hezekiah quotes here. Cp. 2 Kings 19. 25, 26. Isa. 37. 27. groweth up. Either unsheatheth itself into flower, or is plucked up (as Sept. and Vulg.).
129:8 | name. See note on 20. 1.
130:1 | 130 (G2/11, p. 826). GROUP IV. PSALM (SECOND). TRUST. (Alternation.)
130:1 | Title. A Song of degrees. Same as 120. See Ap. 67.
130:1 | depths. Symbolical of distress. Cp. 42. 7; 66. 12; 69. 2. LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II.
130:2 | LORD*. One of the 134 places where the Sopherim altered Jehovah to Adonai. See Ap. 32. So also vv. 3, and 6. ears. Fig. Anthropopatheia. Ap. 6.
130:3 | LORD. Heb. Jah. Ap. 4. III.
130:4 | But = For; or Because; corresponding with v. 7. forgiveness = the forgiveness: viz. that which Hezekiah gave thanks for in Isa. 38. 17.
130:5 | soul. Heb. nephesh. Ap. 13. His word: as sent to Hezekiah by Jehovah through Isaiah.
130:6 | watch. Heb. shamar = to keep = observe. An astronomical word, as in 19. 11. Cp. 105. 45; 107. 43; 119. 34. Omit the italics, note the Fig. Epizeuxis. Ap. 6, and render: "More than watchers for the morning [while] watching for the morning."
130:7 | mercy = lovingkindness, or grace. plenteous redemption. Not only from the king of Assyria (Isa. 37), but from "the king of terrors". redemption. Heb. padah. See notes on Ex. 13. 13.
130:8 | redeem. Same as v. 7. iniquities. Heb. 'avah. Ap. 44. iv. (Isa. 38.)
131:1 | 131 (G2/12, p. 826). GROUP IV. PSALM (THIRD) DELIVERANCE, BLESSING, AND PEACE. (Introversion.)
131:1 | Title. A Song of degrees. Same as 120. Ap. 67. of David = by David. For its place here see Ap. 67.
131:1 | LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II. high = wonderful.
131:2 | Surely = [See] whether I have not, &c. quieted = silenced. Some codices, with Sept. and Vulg., read "soothed and uplifted": i. e. comforted. myself = my soul. Heb. nephesh. Ap. 13. My soul = Myself. Heb. nephesh. Ap. 13.
131:3 | Israel. Not "a liturgical addition", but used to link the three Psalms of this group together (129. 1; 130. 7, 8; 131. 3). For Israel is to find rest and peace where their kings (David, and Hezekiah) found it.
132:1 | 132 (G2/13, p. 826). GROUP V. PSALM (FIRST). DISTRESS. BLESSING FOR THE HOUSE OF JEHOVAH. (Extended Alternation.)
132:1 | The Theme. David's Distress.
132:1 | Title. A Song of degrees. Same as 120. Ap. 67.
132:1 | LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II. remember David = remember for David: i. e. remember to fulfil the promises made to him. David. Hezekiah remembers David, and puts Jehovah in remembrance of him. all his afflictions = all his being afflicted: all his anxious cares as to his work. The Temple was Hezekiah's care, as it had been David's. Not "post-exilic". We see these anxieties from the beginning of his reign (2 Sam. 7. 1 Chron. 13. 3; 21. 18—22. 1). The subjects of these last three Psalms are merged in blessing.
132:2 | the mighty [God] of Jacob. The mighty One to Whom Jacob vowed his vow. Title occurs outside the Pentateuch, only here, and Gen. 49. 24; Isa. 1. 24 (Israel); 49. 26; 60. 16. Note the Fig. Epistrophe (Ap. 6) for emphasis in v. 5.
132:3 | I will not come. Note the Fig. Periphrases (Ap. 6) in vv. 4, 5. tabernacle = tent. Heb. 'ohel. Ap. 40. 3. Cp. Acts 7. 46. of. Gen. of Apposition = "the Tent: i. e. my house". The emphasis = my own house. bed = couch.
132:5 | An habitation. Pl. of Majesty. Heb. mishkan (Ap. 40. ii).
132:6 | heard: i. e. while he was at Ephratah. David's father was an Ephrathite (of Bethlehem-Ephratah. Cp. Gen. 35. 19. David had "heard" of it as being in Shiloh. it: i. e. the Ark. in the fields of the wood = at Jaar's fields: i. e. Kirjath-jearim (1 Chron. 13. 5.).
132:7 | tabernacles = the plural of Majesty. His great habitation. Heb. Mishkan. Ap. 40. 2. footstool. Fig. Anthropopatheia. Ap. 6.
132:8 | Arise. Fig. Ellipsis (Ap. 6) = [and will say] "Arise, O LORD", &c. This is what Solomon did say in 2 Chron. 6. 41: see Ps. 68. 1 (and note there), according to Num. 10. 35. Verses 8-10 record what David said. the ark of Thy strength. Occurs only here and 2 Chron. 6. 41. See notes on Ex. 25. 22 and 1 Chron. 13. 3.
132:9 | saints = favoured ones. shout for joy. That is exactly what they did. See the sub-scription of Ps. 87 and note there on "Mahalath-Leannoth".
132:10 | Thine anointed = i. e. David. Not "Zerubbabel", no such oath made to him.
132:11 | hath sworn. See 2 Sam. 7. 8-17. in truth = a truth. Of the fruit of thy body. This was what Hezekiah was concerned about; for he as yet had no son, and was in danger of death. Hence this pleading of Jehovah's oath to David. Quoted in Acts 2. 30.
132:12 | children = sons.
132:12 | My testimony. Heb. = "this My testimony". Some codices, with Aram., Sept., and Vulg., read "[these] my testimonies" (pl.). children = sons.
132:13 | Zion. See Ap. 68. His habitation = His dwelling. Fig. Anthropopatheia (Ap. 6).
132:15 | her: i. e. Zion's. poor = needy ones.
132:17 | the horn of David = a horn for David. to bud: i. e. to bring forth: viz. a son and heir. See Ap. 67. xiv. lamp. According to Gen. 15. 17; and note there.
132:18 | his crown: i. e. his royal crown.
133:1 | 133 (G2/14, p. 826). GROUP V. PSALM (SECOND). TRUST. BLESSING IN THE HOUSE OF JEHOVAH. (Introversion and Alternation.)
133:1 | Title. A Song of degrees. Same as 120. The three subjects of the three Psalms of this last group are merged in blessing. of David = by David. Hezekiah found this Psalm exactly suited for his purpose. David wrote it on the experience of a similar blessing of "unity," when "all Israel" were united "as the heart of One Man" (2 Sam. 19. 9, 14). It was the same with Hezekiah. Read 2 Chron. 30. 5, 6, 11, 18, and note the "One Heart" (v. 12). See Ap. 67. xv.
133:1 | Behold. The word of the Holy Spirit; as "yea" is of the Father; and "verily" of the Son. Note the Fig. Asterismos. Ap. 6. how good. This was manifested in 2 Chron. 30. 25, 26. unity = one. The reference is to the "one man" of 2 Sam. 19. 14 (David), and the "one heart" of 2 Chron. 30. 12 (Hezekiah). Heb. yahad (not 'ehad. See note on Deut. 6. 4. Cp. Josephus (Ant. ix. 13. 2).
133:2 | ointment = oil (Ex. 30. 23-25). ran down = descended. Ex. 29. 7. Lev. 8. 12; 21. 10. went down = descended, as in v. 3. to the skirts, &c. = to the opening of his robes (see Ex. 28. 32). Heb. = mouth (or opening).
133:3 | As = [It is] like, as in v. 2. And as the dew. Omit these italics. descended. Cp. v. 2. Zion. The dew (or copious summer night mist) was one. The same dew descended on Zion in the south as on Hermon in the north. Zion's dew represents the tribe of Judah. Hermon's dew represents Asher, Ephraim, Manasseh, Zebulun, Issachar (2 Chron. 30. 11, 18, 25, 26). The idea is not in the motion of this dew, from Hermon to Zion, but in its uniting both in its copious descent. there. Cp. Deut. 12. 5, 11, 14, 18, 21. Pss. 128. 5; 134. 3. Ps. 133 is blessing in Zion; Ps. 132 is blessing for Zion; Ps. 134 is blessing from Zion. the LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II.
134:1 | 134 (G2/15, p. 826). GROUP V. PSALM (THIRD). BLESSING FROM THE HOUSE OF JEHOVAH. (Introversion.)
134:1 | Title. A Song of degrees. Same as 120. See Ap. 67.
134:1 | Behold. See note on 133. 1. the LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II. servants. Limited and defined in next clause, as in 135. 2. stand. The night-watchmen. The reference is to 2 Chron. 29. 11; 30. 16; 31. 2. There were no seats in the Tabernacle or Temple. Cp. Heb. 10. 11. the house of the LORD. The reference is to Hezekiah's interest in the Temple. See Ap. 67. xiii.
134:3 | heaven and earth. The reference is to 2 Chron. 32. 19. 2 Kings 19. 15. Isa. 37. 16. See Ap. 67. v. Bless thee. The reference may be to 2 Chron. 30. 27; 31. 10. This is the last of the fifteen Songs of the Degrees, which are referred to in Isa. 38. 20. See Ap. 67. out of Zion. See note on "there", 133. 3.
135:1 | 135 (H2/1, p. 826). PRAISE. (Introversion and Alternation.)
135:1 | This Psalm is probably by Hezekiah, continuing the Songs of the Degrees. Corresponds with 114 and 115 (H1). See Structure (p. 826).
135:1 | Praise ye THE LORD = Hallelu-jah. Ap. 4. III. name. See note on 20. 1. the LORD. Heb. Jehovah (Ap. 4. II). Note the three Jehovahs between Jah in v. 1 and Elohim in v. 2. Corresponding with the threefold blessing of Num. 6. 22-27.
135:2 | the courts. This includes the People as well as the priests and Levites. God. Heb. Elohim. Ap. 4. I.
135:3 | it: i. e. His name.
135:4 | Jacob. Cp. Mal. 1. 2. Rom. 9. 13. Put also by Fig. Metonymy (of Cause) for his posterity (Ap. 6). Israel. See notes on Gen. 32. 28; 43. 6; 45. 26, 28. peculiar treasure = own possession. Heb. segullah. See note on Ex. 19. 5.
135:5 | our LORD = Adonim. Ap. 4. VIII (3). gods. Heb. 'elohim. Ap. 4. I. Used here of earthly rulers (82. 6), as representing God. See note on Ex. 22. 9, and cp. Rom. 13. 1-7.
135:6 | heaven, and in earth. Hezekiah's expression. See Ap. 67. v.
135:7 | wind. Heb. ruach. Ap. 9. Cp. v. 7 with Jer. 10. 13; 51. 16. treasuries. Heb. = treasures, put by Fig. Metonymy (of Adjunct) for treasuries (Ap. 6), and rightly so rendered. Cp. Job 38. 22.
135:8 | smote, &c. Cp. Ex. 12. 29. man. Heb. 'adam. Ap. 14. I.
135:9 | wonders. Cp. Ex. 7—14, and Ps. 136. 15.
135:10 | smote, &c. Cp. Num. 21—26, 34, and 35.
135:11 | Sihon. Cp. Num. 21. 21-34. Deut. 1. 4. Og. Cp. Deut. 31. 4. Josh. 13. 31. all. Cp. Josh. 12. 7.
135:12 | gave their land. Cp. Josh. 12. 7. An heritage. Repeated by Fig. Anadiplosis. Ap. 6.
135:13 | Thy name. Cp. v. 13 with Ex. 3. 15.
135:14 | For, &c. Cp. Deut. 32. 36. judge = vindicate. repent Himself = have compassion.
135:15 | 15-18 (W, above). SUPERIORITY OVER IDOLS. (Introversion.)
135:15 | The idols, &c., vv. 15-18. Not "borrowed" from Ps. 115, but repeated, and varied, because the object here is quite different. Ps. 115 = heathen theology; 135 = Divine theology. heathen = nations.
135:17 | breath. Heb. ruach. Ap. 9.
135:18 | are = will become. trusteth = confideth. Heb. batah. Ap. 69. I.
135:19 | house of Israel. Includes all Israel. Cp. 115. 12. See note on Ex. 16. 31.
135:20 | Levi. Not included in 115.
135:21 | out of Zion. Shows that this Psalm is an expansion of Ps. 134. dwelleth. Fig. Anthropopatheia. Ap. 6.
136:1 | 136 (H2/2, p. 826). PRAISE. (Introversion and Extended Alternation.)
136:1 | Psalms 135 and 136 are a pair; and have a corresponding Structure when viewed together. This does not interfere with their own independent Structures. In Ps. 135, verses 19-22 are not "an addition" or "interpolation" from 135. 10, in order to make twenty-two verses (the number of letters in the Hebrew alphabet). Without these verses (vv. 19-22) the Structure below would fail.
136:1 | PSALMS 135 AND 136 COMPARED. (Extended Alternation.)
136:1 | the LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II. For, &c. Figs. Amoebaeon and Epistrophe. Ap. 6. mercy = lovingkindness, or grace.
136:2 | O give thanks. Note the Figs. Coenotes and Anaphora (Ap. 6) in vv. 1, 2, 3. God of gods. Heb. Elohim of the elohim. Ap. 4. I. gods. Heb. 'elohim. See note on 135. 5 and Ex. 22. 9.
136:3 | LORD of lords. Heb. Adonim of the adonim. Ap. 4. VIII. Cp. Deut. 10. 17.
136:5 | made the heavens. Cp. Gen. 1. 1.
136:6 | above. i. e. in Gen. 1. 1, and 2 Pet. 3. 5. By the overthrow of Gen. 1. 2 the earth became a ruin, being "overflowed" (2 Pet. 3. 6), and covered with "the deep" (Gen. 1. 2).
136:7 | great lights. Gen. 1. 14, "lightholders".
136:8 | to rule = to have dominion. Gen. 1. 16-18.
136:10 | smote Egypt. Cp. Ex. 12. 29.
136:11 | brought out Israel. Cp. Ex. 13. 17.
136:12 | hand . . . arm. Fig. Anthropopatheia (Ap. 6).
136:15 | overthrew = shook off.
136:19 | Sihon, &c. These two verses (vv. 19, 20) not an "interpolation". See note above.
136:21 | their: i. e. Sihon's and Og's. Not a verse "clearly dropped out", which contained the noun for this pronoun. The kings named show "whose" land is referred to. An "interpolation" from which a verse has "dropped out" is a new idea in the field of imaginative criticism; and, if true, would be quite unworthy of a "commentator's" time and trouble.
136:24 | redeemed = rescued. Heb. parak = to break. Thus to rescue, by breaking the bonds. Rendered "redeem" only here (and Dan. 4. 27 in the Vulgate versions: A.V. "break off"). enemies = adversaries.
136:25 | food. Heb. = bread. Put by Fig. Synecdoche (of Species) for all kinds of food. Ap. 6. flesh. Put by Fig. Synecdoche (of Part) for all living beings. Ap. 6.
136:26 | GOD. Heb. El. Ap. 4. IV. GOD of heaven. See note on 2 Chron. 36. 23.
137:1 | 137 (G3, p. 826). ISRAEL. DELIVERANCE FROM BABYLON. (Repeated Alternation.)
137:1 | Babylon. The Psalm is anonymous, and probably by Hezekiah. No need to refer it to post-exilic times. The Psalm reads as though it were a reminiscence of past experience in Babylon, and a contrast with previous joys in Zion; not, as during or after the seventy years, or an experience of a then present exile in Babylon. The writer is in Jerusalem after an absence not of long duration; and is full of joy. The post-exilic captives were full of sorrow on their return (Ezra 3. 12. Hag. 2. 3). These exiles had obeyed Isaiah's call (Isa. 48. 20. Cp. 43. 14-21).
137:3 | they that carried us away: i. e. the captives of Judah, as those of Israel had been by Shalmaneser and Sargon. The latter took away only 27,280 from Samaria. See note on 1 Chron. 5. 6; and Ap. 67. xi.
137:4 | the LORD'S. Heb. Jehovah's. Ap. 4. II. strange = foreigner's.
137:5 | If I forget . . . do not remember. The writer's then present personal declaration. Let my right hand forget. Supply "me" for the Ellipsis. Some codices, with Sept. and Vulg., read "let my right hand be forgotten".
137:7 | children = sons. Edom. Gen. 27. 39, 40 was not fulfilled until the reign of Joram (2 Kings 8. 20-23. 2 Chron. 21. 8-10 (cp. 1 Kings 22. 47). From that time they were implacable enemies. day. Put by Fig. Metonymy (of Adjunct) for what happened at the time (Ap. 6). Cp. Job 18. 20. Hos. 1. 11. Joel 1. 15. Luke 17. 22, 26; 19. 42. 1 Cor. 4. 3. Who said. The reference is to what they said, not to what they did; to the encouragement given to Sennacherib, not to the help given to Nebuchadnezzar. That is what Obadiah, a later prophet, refers to. Isaiah (Hezekiah's contemporary) refers to the earlier words. See Isa. 34. 6. Here Edom does not go beyond words. Rase it, rase it. Fig. Epizeuxis (Ap. 6) for emphasis. This was said, not done, at that time.
137:8 | to be destroyed. Hezekiah must have been familiar with Isaiah's prophecies, who employs the very words of vv. 8, 9. (Isa. 13. 6, 16-18; 21. 9; 47. 14, 15. Cp. Nahum 3. 10.) Happy. See Ap. 63. VI. for the Beatitudes of the Psalms. rewardeth. See notes above, which show that the "post-exilic" assumption involves insuperable difficulties if this Psalm is sundered from the contemporary prophecies of Isaiah (especially 13. 1-14; and 27), and from a Babylon under Assyrian rule.
137:9 | little ones. The reference is to Isa. 13. 16-18, which belongs to a Dispensation of Law and Judgment, and is not to be interpreted of the present Dispensation of Grace.
138:1 | 138 (H3, p. 826). PRAISE. (Repeated Alternation.)
138:1 | Title. of David = by David. Placed here by Hezekiah, to correspond with H1, H2 and H4. See p. 826.
138:1 | praise Thee. Some codices, with Aram., Sept., Syr., and Vulg., add "O Jehovah". heart. Sept. adds "for Thou hast heard the words of my mouth", probably from v. 4. gods. Heb. 'elohim. Ap. 4. I. See note on Ex. 22. 8.
138:2 | holy. See note on Ex. 3. 5. temple = house or palace. Heb. heykal. name. See note on 20. 1. magnified: i. e. by fulfilling it beyond all expectation. word = sayings. Heb. 'imrah. See Ap. 73. v. name. See note on 20. 1.
138:3 | strengthenedst = encouraged, or emboldened. my soul = myself (emphatic). Heb. nephesh. Ap. 13.
138:4 | LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II. they hear = they have heard.
138:5 | in: or, of.
138:6 | respect = regard.
138:7 | revive = make alive, or sustain in life. See note 11 on p. 827. hand. Some codices, with two early printed editions and Sept., read "hands" (pl.). Fig. Anthropopatheia (Ap. 6). against. Some codices, with two early printed editions, read "Yea, because of".
138:8 | mercy = lovingkindness, or grace. works. Some codices, with Syr., read "work" (sing.). To the chief Musician. See Ap. 64.
139:1 | 139 (G4, p. 826). DELIVERANCE FROM SELF. (Alternation.)
139:1 | Title. A Psalm. Heb. mizmor. Ap. 65. XVII. of David = by David. The words alleged to be Chaldaisms in vv. 3, 4, 8, 20, are found in the earlier books such as Lev. 1 and 2 Sam. There is no internal evidence of non-Davidic authorship.
139:1 | LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II. searched = search out as for treasures or secrets. known = seen, so as to understand.
139:2 | downsitting . . . uprising. Fig. Synecdoche (of Species), Ap. 6, put for all movements. thought = inward thought. Occurs only here and in v. 17.
139:3 | compassest = scrutinisest. lying down = bed. art acquainted with = well knowest, or hast inspected.
139:4 | altogether = on every side, or, the whole of it.
139:5 | behind and before. Fig. Synecdoche (of Species), Ap. 6, put for every direction.
139:7 | spirit. Heb. ruach. Ap. 9. presence. Heb. = face. Fig. Anthropopatheia. Ap. 6.
139:8 | hell = Sheol. See Ap. 35. behold. Fig. Asterismos. Ap. 6.
139:9 | wings of the morning. See note on sub-scription of Ps. 21.
139:10 | right hand. Fig. Anthropopatheia. Ap. 6.
139:12 | hideth not from = cannot be too dark for.
139:13 | covered = woven me together. Cp. Job 10. 8, 11.
139:14 | fearfully and wonderfully. Heb. = fears and wonders. Put by Fig. Metonymy (of Cause), Ap. 6, for the feelings produced by the works. my soul = myself. Heb. nephesh. Ap. 13.
139:15 | substance = frame. Heb. = bone, as in Gen. 2. 21, 22. curiously = skilfully. Heb. = embroidered. Cp. Ex. 26. 1; 35. 35.
139:16 | substance . . . unperfect = unfinished substance. Not the same word as in v. 15. One word in Hebrew. unperfect. Not imperfect. Which in continuance = the days which were ordered, or in which they should be fashioned.
139:17 | How precious. Fig. Ecphonesis. Ap. 6. See note on 1 Sam. 3. 1. thoughts = desires. See v. 2. GOD. Heb. El. Ap. 4. IV. sum. Heb. pl. of majesty, denoting the fullness or vastness of them.
139:18 | more in number . . . sand. Fig. Paroemia. Ap. 6.
139:19 | Surely, &c. These six verses (19-24) are not an "interpolation". They are required to complete the Structure. See above. Thou wilt slay, &c. This is Omnipotent work. Cp. Job 40. 9-14. the wicked = a lawless one. Heb. rasha'. Ap. 44. x. GOD. Heb. Eloah. Ap. 4. V. bloody = bloodthirsty. men. Heb. 'enosh. Ap. 14. III.
139:20 | wickedly = rebelliously (pre-meditated).
139:21 | am not I grieved with = do I not loathe.
139:23 | thoughts = distractions or cares. Not the same word as in vv. 2, 17.
139:24 | wicked = painful or grievous. way. Put by Fig. Metonymy (of Cause), Ap. 6, for the grief produced by it. way everlasting. Put by Fig. Metonymy (of Effect), Ap. 6, for the happiness which is the effect and end of everlasting life. To the chief Musician. See Ap. 64.
140:1 | 140 (H4/1, p. 826). PRAYER AND PRAISE. (Repeated Alternation.)

140:1 | LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II. the evil = an evil man. Heb. ra'a'. Ap. 44. viii. man. Heb. 'adam. Ap. 14. I. man. Heb. 'ish. Ap. 14. II.

141:1 | 141 (H4/2, p. 826). PRAYER AND PRAISE. (Introversion and Extended Alternation.)
141:1 | Title. A Psalm of David. See Title of 140.
141:1 | LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II.
141:2 | incense. Cp. Ex. 30. 7. Rev. 8. 3, 4. sacrifice = gift offering. Ap. 43. II. iii.
141:3 | Set a watch = Set a guard. Heb. shamrah. Same word as "keep", v. 9 (A). Occurs only here. Keep = keep in safety. Not the same word as in v. 9.
141:4 | evil. Heb. ra'a'. Ap. 44. viii. wicked. Heb. rasha'. Ap. 44. x. men. Heb. 'ish. Ap. 14. II. iniquity. Heb. 'aven. Ap. 44. iii. eat: i.e. partake of, or have fellowship with. dainties = pleasant things. Cp. v. 6.
141:5 | the righteous = a righteous one. an excellent oil = oil for the head. This verse is said to be "extremely obscure" and "corrupt to a degree". The Fig. Metalepsis (Ap. 6) makes all clear; "head", being first used for hair, and then for the whole person by Fig. Synecdoche (of the Part). Heb. = "as oil on the hair, I will not refuse it". Note the alternation of lines in this verse. yet. Same root as "withal" in v. 10 (B).
141:6 | When. Not in Hebrew text. judges = rulers. Cp. 2 Kings 9. 33. in stony places = as by a rock; or, over a rock. Heb. by the hands of a rock. They: i.e. the people. words = sayings. Heb. 'imrah. Ap. 73. v. sweet. Cognate with "dainties", v. 4.
141:7 | Our bones. Sept. (Vatican B, and Alex. A by second hand), Syr., Arab., and Ethiop. read "their bones" the grave's. Heb. Sheol's. Ap. 35. Note the word "bones" in this connection. cutteth = sliceth, as in 1 Sam. 30. 12. Song 4. 3; 6. 7 (elsewhere rendered "piece" or "pieces"). Never means "ploweth", as in R.V. cleaveth. As in Ecc. 10. 9. Zech. 14. 4 (cp. Gen. 22. 3. 1 Sam. 6. 14).
141:8 | GOD the Lord. Heb. Jehovah Adonai. Ap. 4. II. VIII (2). is my trust = have I sought refuge. Heb. hasah. Ap. 69. II. my soul = me (emphatic). Heb. nephesh. Ap. 13.
141:9 | Keep. Same root as "watch" in v. 3. (Heb. shamreni.) snares. A.V., 1611, read "snares" (sing.). Since 1769, "snares" (pl.). Heb. text is sing. gins = traps. Short for "engine" = an ingenious contrivance.
141:10 | the wicked = lawless ones. Heb. rasha'. Ap. 44. x. withal = Same as "yet", v. 5 (B). escape = pass on [in safety].
142:1 | 142 (H4/3, p. 826). PRAYER AND PRAISE. (Extended Alternation.)
142:1 | Title. Maschil = Instruction. See Ap. 65. XI. of David = by David. when . . . cave. Adullam (1 Sam. 22. 1) or En-gedi (1 Sam. 24. 3). The last of eight Psalms referring to this subject.
142:1 | the LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II.

143:1 | 143 (H4/4, p. 826). PRAYER. (Introversion and Alternation.)
143:1 | Title. A Psalm of David. Same as 140.
143:1 | LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II.
143:2 | enter not. As in Job 9. 32; 22. 4. in Thy sight. Cp. 1 Sam. 16. 7. Isa. 55. 8. Job 14. 3. no man = no one. Cp. Job 15. 14, 15. be justified = stand or appear [before Thee] just. The verb is Active. Sept., A.V., and R.V. render it passive. Cp. Rom. 3. 20. Gal. 2. 16.

143:6 | as. Some codices, with seven early printed editions, read "in". Selah. Connecting and returning to prayer (7-11) as the consequence of the reflection (5, 6). This is the last Selah (of seventy-one) in the Psalms. For three others, see Hab. 3. 3, 9, 13. See Ap. 66. II.

144:1 | 144 (H4/5, p. 826). PRAYER AND THANKSGIVING. (Repeated Alternation.)
144:1 | Title. of David = by David. The Sept. adds "concerning Goliath." This may be because Ps. 8, which relates to David and Goliath (see its sub-scription), has the same words in v. 4 as in 144. 3. In any case, Ps. 144 is peculiarly appropriate to David's victory (1 Sam. 17). Not a "compilation" of "fragments" of some "lost Psalms", but a perfect whole with a perfect design, as shown by the Structure above.
144:1 | Blessed. Fig. Benedictio (Ap. 6). Not Beatitudo as in v. 15. the LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II. strength = rock, or fortress. Cp. Deut. 32. 4. 1 Sam. 2. 2; 2 Sam. 22. 47. Pss. 18. 2, 31, 46; 19. 14; 28. 1; 62. 2, 6. to war . . . to fight. Not merely generally, but specially in the case of Goliath (1 Sam. 17). See Title.
144:2 | goodness = lovingkindness. The Syr. reads "refuge". deliverer = liberator. Heb. palat. Not the same word as in vv. 7, 10, 11. See notes below. trust = flee for refuge. Heb. hasah. Ap. 69. II. my People: A special reading called Sevir (see Ap. 34), and some other codices, with Aram. and Syr., read "peoples". Cp. 18. 47. under me. In some codices there is a marginal note: "under Him"; and this is read the text, in some codices.
144:3 | what . . . ? Fig. Erotesis. Ap. 6. Cp. 8. 4. man. Heb. 'adam. Ap. 14. I. Or. This word is read in some codices, with one early printed edition. man. Heb. 'enosh. Ap. 14. III.
144:4 | Man. Heb. 'adam. Ap. 14. I.
144:6 | Cast forth = Flash. Heb. lighten lightnings. Fig. Polyptoton (Ap. 6). See note on Gen. 26. 28. arrows. Fig. Anthropopatheia. Ap. 6.
144:7 | Send = Put forth. hand. Heb. text reads "hands" (pl.); but some codices, with one early printed edition, Aram., Sept., Syr., and Vulg., read "hand" (sing.), which A.V. and R.V. followed. Rid = snatch. Heb. pazah; same word as in v. 11, and "delivereth", v. 10. deliver = pluck, or rescue. Heb. nazal; same word as in v. 11, not the same as in vv. 2, 10. strange. Always means foreign, in Hebrew, as in early English. strange children = aliens. Heb. = 'sons of the foreigner.
144:8 | speaketh. Cp. v. 11; and note the words they speak in vv. 12-15.
144:9 | God. Heb. Elohim. Ap. 4. I.
144:10 | delivereth = snatcheth; same word as "rid", vv. 7, 11. David . . . sword; with special reference to Goliath's sword in 1 Sam. 17. 50, 51.
144:12 | That = Who. Heb. 'asher. Supply the Ellipsis thus: "Who [say] our sons are, &c." All the words in italic type in vv. 12-15- may be omitted, or the Present Tense may be supplied throughout. The verb "say" or "saying" is very frequently to be thus understood. See note on 109. 5. may be. Supply "Are" and omit "That"
144:13 | sheep = flocks. streets = open fields. Heb. that which is outside the house.
144:14 | strong to labour = well laden. no breaking in = no invasion. nor going out = no captivity.
144:15 | Happy. See Ap. 63. VI. in such a case: i.e. holding the false view that happiness consists in outward prosperity. Cp. 4. 6, 7, and 146. 3 and 5. Yea. Supply the Ellipsis (Ap. 6), not as in A.V. and R.V., but [Yea, rather], or [Nay]. The last member (L3) being David's own words; denying the vain and false words of the aliens (vv. 8 and 12-15), and declaring the truth as to that in which real happiness consists. See note on 4. 6, 7.
145:1 | 145 (E, p. 826). DAVID'S [PSALM] OF PRAISE. (Repeated Introversion.)
145:1 | Title. David's [Psalm] of praise. No other Psalm so entitled. An acrostic Psalm. See Ap. 63. VII.
145:1 | 1, 2 (N1, above). DAVID ALONE. (Alternation.)
145:1 | God. Heb. Elohim. Ap. 4. I. name. See note on 20. 1.
145:3 | the LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II.
145:4 | 4, 7 (N2, above). DAVID AND OTHERS. PRAISE. (Alternation.)
145:5 | glorious, &c. Heb. = the majesty of the glory of Thine honour. wondrous works = instances or examples of Thy wonders. Heb. = words of Thy wonders.
145:6 | And men = And they. See Structure (N2, above). terrible. See note on 111. 9.
145:7 | abundantly utter = pour forth.
145:8 | gracious, &c. Cp. Ex. 34. 6, 7. of great mercy = great in lovingkindness, or grace.
145:9 | to all. Sept. reads "to them that wait on Him". tender mercies = compassions.
145:10 | 10-12 (N3, above). THE WORKS PRAISE. (Introversion.)
145:10 | saints = favoured or beloved ones. Cp. 16. 10.
145:11 | power. Sing. of the Heb. word "mighty acts" (v. 4).
145:12 | men. Heb. 'adam (with Art.) = mankind. Ap. 14. I.
145:13 | 13-20 (O3, above). PRAISE OFFERED. (Alternation.)
145:13 | an everlasting kingdom = a kingdom for all ages. Looking backward (eternal, 77. 5) as well as forward (everlasting, 77. 7). These words, "Thy kingdom [O Christ] is an everlasting kingdom", were (up to 1893) to be seen on the wall of one of the largest Mosques in Damascus. Formerly it was a Temple of Rimmon. It was turned into the (Christian) Church of St. John the Baptist by Arcadius, later it was made into a Mosque by Caliph Walid I (705-717). It was destroyed by fire on Oct. 14, 1893, and subsequently rebuilt. (Enc. Brit. vol. 7, p. 785, Camb. (11th) edition.) generations. Following this verse (13) the Primitive Text read: "Faithful is Jehovah in all His words And holy in all His works", the verse beginning with the missing letter נ (Nun) = Neeman (= faithful). It is found in some codices, with Sept., Syr., Vulg., Arabic, and Ethiopic Versions. The Structure (O3, above) thus confirms the Ancient Versions.
145:14 | fall = are ready to fall. raiseth up. Occurs only here and 146. 8.
145:15 | eyes of all wait. Fig. Prosopopoeia. Ap. 6.
145:16 | Thou. This is emphatic in Sept., Syr., and Vulg. Cp. 104. 26. hand. Fig. Anthropopatheia. Ap. 6.
145:17 | holy = gracious.
145:18 | nigh: i.e. nigh to help. Cp. 34. 18; 119. 151. Deut. 4. 7. unto all. Note the Fig. Anadiplosis (Ap. 6) in the repetition, "to all that call" (for emphasis).
145:19 | their cry: i.e. for help in distress.
145:20 | wicked = lawless. Heb. rasha'. Ap. 44. x.
145:21 | let all flesh = all flesh shall, as in v. 10; thus completing and perfecting the Structure above. all flesh. Fig. Synecdoche (of the Part), Ap. 6, put for all men. holy. See note on Ex. 3. 5. ever and ever. Many codices, with one early printed edition, here add:— "And we will bless Jah: From henceforth even for ever, Praise ye Jah." Cp. Ps. 115. 18.
146:1 | 146 (J, p. 827). FIRST HALLELUJAH PSALM. (GENESIS.) (Introversion and Extended Alternation.)
146:1 | The first of the five "Hallelujah" Psalms concluding the whole book; each beginning and ending with this word. The first has Genesis for its subject; the second, Exodus; the third, Leviticus; the fourth, Numbers, and the fifth, Deuteronomy. See the Structure, p. 827, and notes below.
146:1 | Praise ye THE LORD = Hallelu-JAH. Ap. 4. III. Praise. Fig. Apostrophe. Ap. 6. the LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II. O my soul = O I myself (emphatic). Heb. nephesh. Ap. 13.
146:2 | God. Heb. Elohim. Ap. 4. I.
146:3 | trust = confidence. Heb. batah. Ap. 69. I. man. Heb. 'adam. Ap. 14. I. no help = no salvation, or saving help. Cp. 33. 16; 60. 11.
146:4 | His breath, &c. This verse occurs in the Apocrypha (1 Macc. 2. 63); but why is it assumed that this verse is taken from the Book of Maccabees, instead of this verse in Maccabees being taken from this Psalm? breath = spirit. Heb. ruach. Ap. 9. Not the same word as in 150. 6. returneth. See Gen. 2. 7; 3. 19, and cp. Ecc. 12. 7. Ps. 104. 29. earth = ground, or dust. Heb. 'adamah. Not 'erez = the Earth. thoughts = purposes, or plans.
146:5 | Happy. The last of the twenty-seven Beatitudes in the Book of Psalms. See Ap. 63. VI. GOD. Heb. El. Ap. 4. IV. of Jacob: i.e. the God who met Jacob (Gen. 28. 13) when he had nothing (Gen. 32. 10), and deserved nothing (but wrath, Gen. 27), and promised him everything. This title answers to the N.T. title "the God of all grace" (1 Pet. 5. 10). Happy indeed are all they who have this God for their God. Whose. Supply the Ellipsis by repeating [Happy he] whose, &c. hope = expectation.
146:6 | made heaven, and earth. Another reference to Genesis (ch. 1). Cp. v. 4 (above). See notes on Gen. 14. 19 and Deut. 4. 26.
146:7 | food. Heb. bread. Put by Fig. Synecdoche (of Species), Ap. 6, for food in general. the. No Art. in Heb.
146:9 | strangers = aliens. relieveth. Plenty of saving "help" here. Cp. the contrast with "man", (d, v. 3), "no help". wicked = lawless. Heb. rasha'. Ap. 44. x.
146:10 | for ever. Contrast v. 4. Cp. Rev. 11. 15. Zion. See Ap. 68.
147:1 | 147 [For Structure see next page].
147:1 | The second of these five Hallelujah Psalms, the Exodus Psalm.
147:1 | Praise ye THE LORD. Heb. Hallelu-JAH. Ap. 4. III. Not "inserted by mistake in verse 1 instead of in the title", but required here by the Structure. See the Structure. For. Not "come into the first line from the second by dittography", but an essential part of the second line, which is repeated by the Fig. Anadiplosis (Ap. 6) in the third line. The first verse thus does contain two lines (beside the "Hallelujah") like all the other verses. God. Heb. Elohim. Ap. 4. I.
147:1 | 147 (K, p. 827). THE SECOND HALLELUJAH PSALM. (EXODUS.) (Introversion and Extended Alternation.)
147:2 | The LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II. doth build up Jerusalem = is Jerusalem's builder (participle). No reference to post-exilic building. Cp. 122. 3. gathereth = will gather. outcasts = the driven away.
147:4 | stars . . . names. See Ap. 12. Cp. Isa. 40. 26. names. The reference is to the knowledge of the "names" in building up the nation of Israel. Cp. vv. 2 and 20 with Ex. 1. 7-20; and vv. 15, 19 with Ex. 20.
147:5 | our LORD. Heb. 'Adonim. Ap. 4. VIII (3). Cp. 135. 5. of great power = abounding in power. Cp. Isa. 40. 26.
147:6 | the meek = meek or humble ones. Num. 12. 3. wicked = lawless. Heb. rasha'. Ap. 44. x.
147:8 | mountains. The Sept. adds "and herb for the service of men". From thence it found its way into the Prayer Book Version. Cp. 104. 14. Here, the addition is out of place, as "man" is not introduced till v. 10.
147:10 | He taketh not. Some codices, with Sept., Syr., and Vulg., read "Nor taketh delight". man. Heb. 'ish. Ap. 4. II.
147:11 | hope in His mercy = wait for His lovingkindness.
147:12 | Praise the LORD. Not the same word as in v. 1. Used only by David and Solomon. Zion. See Ap. 68. Zion then still standing.
147:13 | children = sons.
147:14 | peace in thy borders = thy borders peace.
147:15 | commandment = sayings, or utterances. Heb. 'imrah. See Ap. 73. v. Cp. 33. 9; 107. 20.
147:18 | word. Heb. dabar = the subject-matter of the articulate utterance (v. 15). See Ap. 73. x. wind. Heb. ruach. Ap. 9.
147:19 | word. Some codices, with Sept., Syr., and Vulg., read "word". Others read plural. Jacob . . . Israel. See notes on Gen. 32. 28; cp. 43. 6; 45. 26, 28.
147:20 | not dealt so, &c. The reference is to Exod. 20. Cp. Deut. 4. 7, 8; and see Ap. 15. they have not known them. Sept. and Vulg. read "He maketh not known to them".
148:1 | 148 (L, p. 827). THE THIRD HALLELUJAH PSALM. (LEVITICUS.) (Introversion and Extended Alternation.)
148:1 | The third of the last five Hallelujah Psalms. The Leviticus Psalm. Cp. v. 14 with Lev. 10. 3.
148:1 | Praise ye THE LORD. Hallelu-JAH. Ap. 4. III. the LORD. Heb. Jehovah with 'eth = Jehovah Himself. Ap. 4. II. heights. As in Job 16. 19; 25. 2.
148:3 | stars of light. Gen. of Origin (Ap. 17. 2) = stars that give light = light-bearers, as in Gen. 1. 14-16.
148:6 | which shall not pass: or, which [they] shall not pass.
148:7 | the earth. Note the change and see Structure above. dragons = sea monsters.
148:8 | wind. Heb. ruach. Ap. 9.
148:11 | people = peoples.
148:13 | name. See note on 20. 1. earth and heaven: i.e. combining the two subjects of vv. 1 and 7. This order of these two words occurs only here and Gen. 2. 4. Cp. note on Deut. 4. 26.
148:14 | saints = favoured ones, or beloved. children = sons. near unto Him. The reference is to Leviticus and its leading thought. See Lev. 10. 3; 21. 21; and cp. 65. 4.
149:1 | 149 (K, p. 827). THE FOURTH HALLELUJAH PSALM. (NUMBERS.) (Introversion and Alternation.)
149:1 | The fourth of the concluding five Hallelujah Psalms, answering to Numbers. Cp. vv. 5-9 with Num. 24. 17-24.
149:1 | Praise ye THE LORD. Heb. Hallelu-JAH. Ap. 4. III. the LORD. Heb. Jehovah. Ap. 4. II. congregation = assembly (in its military aspect). saints = favoured ones, or beloved, v. 5 (W).
149:2 | Israel. Cp. the Structure. children = sons. Zion. See Ap. 68.
149:3 | In the Hebrew this verse is an Introversion:— name. See note on 20. 1. timbrel = drum. See note on Ex. 15. 20.
149:4 | taketh pleasure. Cp. Isa. 54. 7, 8.
149:5 | in glory = "with [ascriptions of] glory". beds = couches.
149:6 | high praises = extollings. GOD. Heb. El. Ap. 4. IV.
149:7 | heathen = nations. people = peoples.
149:9 | the judgment written. See Deut. 32. 40-43. Cp. Isa. 45. 14. Ezek. 25. 14; chs. 38 and 39. Zech. 14. The special reference is to Numbers 24. 17-24.
150:1 | 150 (J, p. 827). THE FIFTH HALLELUJAH PSALM. (DEUTERONOMY.) (Introversion.)
150:1 | The fifth of the last five Hallelujah Psalms, answering to the great thought of Deuteronomy. Cp. v. 2 with Deut. 3. 24, and 32. 43.
150:1 | Praise ye THE LORD. Heb. Hallelu-JAH. Ap. 4. III. GOD. Heb. El. Ap. 4. IV. sanctuary. The earthly sanctuary and the heavenly: the lower being formed on the pattern of the higher. See Heb. 8. 5; 9. 23; and cp. 1 Chron. 28. 11-13, 19. firmament: Heb. = expanse (Gen. 1. 6).
150:2 | for = in [the recital of] His mighty acts. mighty acts. Some codices, with Aram. and Syr., read "His might". excellent = the abundance of His greatness or grandeur.
150:3 | the. No Art. in Heb. text.
150:4 | timbrel = drum. See note on Ex. 15. 20. organs = pipe, or reed (sing., never pl.).
150:6 | breath. Heb. neshamah (see Ap. 16): i.e. in contrast with material instruments. praise THE LORD. Heb. tehallel jah. Praise ye THE LORD. Heb. Halelujah, thus fitly closing the Book of Psalms. Cp. the endings of the other four books; and see notes on p. 720.
